            statement=(
                "The residential feeder meter publishes the mean power served "
                "to the feeder over the span that just ended."
            ),
        ),
        DeviceSignalDeclaration(
            device_id="clinic-meter",
            signal_id="active-power",
            state_key="load-served-power",
            reading_class="INTERVAL_SIGNAL",
            canonical_unit="kW",
            cadence_minutes=15,
            bias=0.0,
            dropout_per_thousand=0,
            statement=(
                "The clinic meter publishes the mean power served to the "
                "clinic over the span that just ended."
            ),
        ),
        DeviceSignalDeclaration(
            device_id="mill-meter",
            signal_id="active-power",
            state_key="load-served-power",
            reading_class="INTERVAL_SIGNAL",
            canonical_unit="kW",
            cadence_minutes=15,
            bias=0.0,
            dropout_per_thousand=0,
            statement=(
                "The mill meter publishes the mean power served to the mill "
                "over the span that just ended."
            ),
        ),
    ),
)

#: ## Version four since T025b, beside version three rather than over it
#:
#: Two generator reporting paths change, and a run of an unchanged document
#: would generate a different series, so the version moves. Unlike three, four
#: is ADDED: version three stays registered and stays executable, so a run
#: frozen under it keeps its readings, digests and stored readback
#: (`D-2026-09-29-t025-generator-policy-package`: publication profile 4 moves
#: only when its contract changes, with prior readers kept).
#:
#: - `generator-controller/status` is new: the generator's lifecycle member
#:   (`STOPPED`/`STARTING`/`RUNNING`/`FAILED`) as a typed discrete reading in
#:   unit `state`, sampled at T after that instant's due transitions. No bias,
#:   noise or quantization - an enum refuses them - and one sample in
#:   twenty-five does not arrive, so a missing status and a retained (STALE)
#:   one are visible in an ordinary run.
#: - `generator-controller/ac-power` publishes every fifteen minutes rather than
#:   hourly, reporting the mean output over the completed step. It reports only
#:   for a model that measures generator output over the interval (the fuel
#:   model, `mini-grid` v2).
#: - `generator-status-reporting-availability` lets a scenario silence the
#:   status path across a window. Like the fuel path's, it is a condition on
#:   the path and moves nothing physical.
#:
#: Generator status is not staged: envelope schema 1 carries numbers only, the
#: gateway's allowlist stays fuel-only, and a mapping promising an enum under
#: schema 1 is refused. T026 owns staging it.
LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V4 = replace(
    LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE,
    publication_profile_version=4,
    statement=(
        LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE.statement
        + " Version four adds the generator controller's typed status reading "
        "and publishes its output power every fifteen minutes; generator status "
        "is observed here and not yet staged."
    ),
    supported_reporting_states=LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE.supported_reporting_states
    + (
        SupportedReportingState(
            state_key="generator-status-reporting-availability",
            scope="COMPONENT",
            supported_roles=frozenset({"FORCING_INPUT"}),
            statement=(
                "Whether the generator controller's status reporting path is "
                "carrying readings can be forced across a declared window. It "
                "changes nothing the generator does and nothing its controller "
                "decides; it changes which status readings exist."
            ),
            silences=(("generator-controller", "status"),),
        ),
    ),
    device_signals=tuple(
        replace(
            declaration,
            cadence_minutes=15,
            statement=(
                "The generator controller publishes its mean output over the "
                "completed step every fifteen minutes, with no measurement "
                "error and losing no sample. The mean is the accepted energy "
                "the fuel was debited for, divided by the span."
            ),
        )
        if declaration.signal_key == ("generator-controller", "ac-power")
        else declaration
        for declaration in LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE.device_signals
    )
    + (
        DeviceSignalDeclaration(
            device_id="generator-controller",
            signal_id="status",
            state_key="generator-state",
            reading_class="STATE_SIGNAL",
            canonical_unit="state",
            cadence_minutes=15,
            bias=0.0,
            dropout_per_thousand=40,
            statement=(
                "The generator controller publishes the generator's lifecycle "
                "state every fifteen minutes as the member it is - STOPPED, "
                "STARTING, RUNNING or FAILED - sampled at that instant after the "
                "transitions already due there, so a start the controller "
                "requests at that instant is not in it. One sample in "
                "twenty-five does not arrive. It is not staged: T026 owns that."
            ),
        ),
    ),
)

#: ## Version five since T026, beside three and four
#:
#: The dispatch-capable profile. Four changes, each reaching new Drafts only:
#:
#: - two capability signals, `pv-inverter-controller/available-power` and
#:   `battery-pcs-controller/discharge-capability`, mean kW over the completed
#:   step. Configured by template v3 only; a model that does not measure them
#:   (anything before `mini-grid` v3) makes them no reporting path at all, so
#:   capability is left unreported rather than inferred;
#: - generator status is STAGED: the gateway mapping profile 5 publishes
#:   through carries it as a typed member under envelope schema 2;
#: - record channels: the Foundation policy the controller runs under, and
#:   operator override commands;
#: - four reporting-path states: a discharge-capability gap, a missing override
#:   record, and a publication delay on the state of charge and on override
#:   records.
LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V5 = replace(
    LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V4,
    publication_profile_version=5,
    statement=(
        LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE.statement
        + " Version five publishes everything version four observes - "
        "generator status included, as a typed member - plus the PV inverter's "
        "available power and the battery's discharge capability, the frozen "
        "Foundation policy and operator override commands as records; and a "
        "run may declare a capability reporting gap, a missing override record "
        "or a delayed publication."
    ),
    supported_reporting_states=LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V4.supported_reporting_states
    + (
        SupportedReportingState(
            state_key="battery-discharge-capability-reporting-availability",
            scope="COMPONENT",
            supported_roles=frozenset({"FORCING_INPUT"}),
            statement=(
                "Whether the battery management system's discharge-capability "
                "path is carrying readings can be forced across a declared "
                "window. Nothing about the battery changes; no capability is "
                "substituted for the readings that do not exist."
            ),
            silences=(("battery-pcs-controller", "discharge-capability"),),
        ),
        SupportedReportingState(
            state_key="override-record-reporting-availability",
            scope="COMPONENT",
            supported_roles=frozenset({"FORCING_INPUT"}),
            statement=(
                "Whether an override command record reaches the gateway can be "
                "forced off across a declared window. The override still holds "
                "in the world; only its record is missing, and a missing record "
                "never says that nothing was commanded."
            ),
            silences=(
                ("generator-controller", "override-command"),
                ("battery-pcs-controller", "override-command"),
            ),
        ),
        SupportedReportingState(
            state_key="battery-soc-publication-delay",
            scope="COMPONENT",
            supported_roles=frozenset({"FORCING_INPUT"}),
            statement=(
                "A state-of-charge publication due in a declared window can be "
                "held back for a declared window: it is published when the "
                "window ends, and there is no separate duration parameter. "
                "Its value and source time are unchanged; only its "
                "publication time moves."
            ),
            delays=(("battery-pcs-controller", "state-of-charge"),),
        ),
        SupportedReportingState(
            state_key="override-record-publication-delay",
            scope="COMPONENT",
            supported_roles=frozenset({"FORCING_INPUT"}),
            statement=(
                "An override command record occurring in a declared window can "
                "be held back for a declared window: it is published when the "
                "window ends, and there is no separate duration parameter. "
                "Its occurrence and effective times are unchanged."
            ),
            delays=(
                ("generator-controller", "override-command"),
                ("battery-pcs-controller", "override-command"),
            ),
        ),
    ),
    device_signals=tuple(
        replace(
            declaration,
            statement=(
                "The generator controller publishes the generator's lifecycle "
                "state every fifteen minutes as the member it is - STOPPED, "
                "STARTING, RUNNING or FAILED - sampled at that instant after the "
                "transitions already due there. One sample in twenty-five does "
                "not arrive. Staged as a typed member, never a number."
            ),
        )
        if declaration.signal_key == ("generator-controller", "status")
        else declaration
        for declaration in LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V4.device_signals
    )
    + (
        DeviceSignalDeclaration(
            device_id="pv-inverter-controller",
            signal_id="available-power",
            state_key="pv-available-power",
            reading_class="INTERVAL_SIGNAL",
            canonical_unit="kW",
            cadence_minutes=15,
            bias=0.0,
            dropout_per_thousand=0,
            statement=(
                "The PV inverter publishes the mean AC power it could have "
                "delivered over the span that just ended - the array's "
                "potential within the inverter's limit - beside what it did "
                "deliver. Capability, not output."
            ),
        ),
        DeviceSignalDeclaration(
            device_id="battery-pcs-controller",
            signal_id="discharge-capability",
            state_key="battery-discharge-capability",
            reading_class="INTERVAL_SIGNAL",
            canonical_unit="kW",
            cadence_minutes=15,
            bias=0.0,
            dropout_per_thousand=0,
            statement=(
                "The battery management system publishes the mean AC power the "
                "PCS could have discharged over the span that just ended: its "
                "limit, or less when the stored energy could not sustain it. It "
                "knows nothing of the Site reserve policy."
            ),
        ),
    ),
    record_channels=(
        ("generator-controller", "policy-start-soc-threshold"),
        ("generator-controller", "policy-stop-soc-threshold"),
        ("generator-controller", "policy-minimum-runtime"),
        ("battery-pcs-controller", "policy-reserve-soc"),
        ("site-controller", "policy-source-priority"),
        ("site-controller", "policy-critical-load-priority"),
        ("generator-controller", "override-command"),
        ("battery-pcs-controller", "override-command"),
    ),
)

#: S2-01b. The registered-Kobo publication profile, version one.
#:
#: `simulator-lab-publication` v3 with one change: every numeric device signal
#: declares the simulated device's published resolution, 0.000001 in its own
#: canonical unit, which is the unit it publishes in. The observation transform
#: rounds the biased reading once, exactly and half-to-even, to that quantum, so
#: every public reading is a finite decimal a SourceEnvelope V3 can carry. A
#: state of charge of 1035/19 percent is published as 54.473684.
#:
#: A NEW identity rather than v6 of the Lab profile, because it is selected only
#: by a registered Site's Draft: run setup refuses it for a legacy YAML Site and
#: refuses it when a declared unit differs from the registered binding's source
#: unit, rather than rounding in a unit the binding does not publish. It is a
#: property of the SIMULATED device, not a claim about a physical Kobo
#: instrument's precision. v3/v4/v5, every Draft frozen against them and every
#: artifact they produced are unchanged.
REGISTERED_KOBO_RESOLUTION = "0.000001"

REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE = replace(
    LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE,
    publication_profile_id="registered-kobo-publication",
    publication_profile_version=1,
    display_name="Registered Kobo publication profile",
    statement=(
        LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE.statement
        + " Each numeric signal is published by a simulated device at a "
        "resolution of 0.000001 in its own unit, rounded half to even after its "
        "bias; that is a property of the simulated device, not of a physical "
        "instrument."
    ),
    device_signals=tuple(
        declaration
        if declaration.canonical_unit == DISCRETE_UNIT
        else replace(declaration, resolution=REGISTERED_KOBO_RESOLUTION)
        for declaration in LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE.device_signals
    ),
)

#: S2-01c. The registered-Kobo publication profile, version two: version one's
#: devices and resolution, plus the state-of-charge publication-delay state
#: (the same declaration `simulator-lab-publication` v5 carries), published
#: through the gateway on the run's virtual publication clock
#: (`D-2026-10-09-s2-simulated-publication-clock`). A report due inside a
#: declared window stays Lab-private and pending - no V3 envelope, no
#: sequence - until the run's publication clock reaches the window's end; the
#: gateway then stamps its actual virtual admission instant, never the
#: scheduled one. Version one, every Draft frozen against it and every
#: envelope it staged are unchanged; a new Draft selects this explicitly.
REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V2 = replace(
    REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE,
    publication_profile_version=2,
    statement=(
        REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE.statement
        + " Version two may hold a state-of-charge publication across a "
        "declared window; the Lab keeps it pending until the run's virtual "
        "publication clock reaches the window's end, and its value and source "
        "time never change."
    ),
    supported_reporting_states=REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE.supported_reporting_states
    + tuple(
        state
        for state in LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V5.supported_reporting_states
        if state.state_key == "battery-soc-publication-delay"
    ),
    publication_clock="RUN_VIRTUAL",
)

#: S3-01 phase 1 (`D-2026-10-11-s3-dispatch-interval-assessment`). The
#: registered-Kobo publication profile, version three: version two's devices,
#: resolution, state-of-charge delay and virtual publication clock, plus what
#: dispatch needs from `simulator-lab-publication` v5 - the generator's typed
#: status reading (STATE_SIGNAL, 15 min), its output power every fifteen
#: minutes (v4's cadence, so status and power share instants), the PV
#: inverter's available power and the PCS's discharge capability (numeric, at
#: the registered resolution) - and the discharge-capability reporting gap.
#: Measurement only: v5's POLICY and override record channels and their
#: states are NOT carried (a registered run publishes V3 measurements, never
#: an invented policy or command record). A capability channel reaches central
#: acceptance only where a `minigrid-roles-2` binding maps it; on a roles-1
#: basis the gateway holds it as an unknown channel. Versions one and two,
#: every Draft frozen against them and every envelope they staged are unchanged.
_V3_FROM_LAB_V5 = (
    ("generator-controller", "status"),
    ("pv-inverter-controller", "available-power"),
    ("battery-pcs-controller", "discharge-capability"),
)
REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V3 = replace(
    REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V2,
    publication_profile_version=3,
    statement=(
        REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V2.statement
        + " Version three also publishes the generator's typed status and its "
        "output power every fifteen minutes, and two simulated capability "
        "readings - the PV inverter's available power and the PCS's discharge "
        "capability - and a run may declare a discharge-capability reporting "
        "gap. It publishes no policy or command record."
    ),
    supported_reporting_states=REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V2.supported_reporting_states
    + tuple(
        state
        for state in LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V5.supported_reporting_states
        if state.state_key == "battery-discharge-capability-reporting-availability"
    ),
    device_signals=tuple(
        replace(
            declaration,
            cadence_minutes=15,
            statement=LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V4.device_signal("generator-controller", "ac-power").statement,
        )
        if declaration.signal_key == ("generator-controller", "ac-power")
        else declaration
        for declaration in REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V2.device_signals
    )
    + tuple(
        declaration
        if declaration.canonical_unit == DISCRETE_UNIT
        else replace(declaration, resolution=REGISTERED_KOBO_RESOLUTION)
        for key in _V3_FROM_LAB_V5
        for declaration in LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V5.device_signals
        if declaration.signal_key == key
    ),
    record_channels=(),
)

MODEL_PROFILES: tuple[ModelProfile, ...] = (
    MINIMAL_FUEL_TANK_MODEL,
    DAYTIME_MINI_GRID_MODEL,
    MINI_GRID_MODEL,
    MINI_GRID_V2_MODEL,
    MINI_GRID_V3_MODEL,
)

PUBLICATION_PROFILES: tuple[PublicationProfile, ...] = (
    LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE,
    LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V4,
    LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V5,
    REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE,
    REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V2,
    REGISTERED_KOBO_PUBLICATION_PROFILE_V3,
)


def find_model_profile(
    profiles: tuple[ModelProfile, ...], profile_id: str, version: int
) -> ModelProfile | None:
    """The one profile with this identity and version, or nothing.

    Identity and version together, never identity alone. A run freezes the
    exact version it was set up against, so resolving "the latest" here would
    make two runs with the same frozen inputs mean different things.
    """
    for profile in profiles:
        if (
            profile.model_profile_id == profile_id
            and profile.model_profile_version == version
        ):
            return profile
    return None


def find_publication_profile(
    profiles: tuple[PublicationProfile, ...], profile_id: str, version: int
) -> PublicationProfile | None:
    """The one publication profile with this identity and version."""
    for profile in profiles:
        if (
            profile.publication_profile_id == profile_id
            and profile.publication_profile_version == version
        ):
            return profile
    return None

 succeeded in 3765ms:
"""The `mini-grid` v1 model: the daytime world plus a generator and its fuel.

T025a, under execution contract 9 and additively
(`D-2026-09-29-t025-generator-policy-package`). `daytime-mini-grid` v1 and
the fuel model are untouched; this pack reuses the daytime pack's PV,
battery, reserve and load machinery and adds:

- an addressed discrete state `generator-state` with the declared members
  STOPPED, STARTING, RUNNING and FAILED. This build starts from STOPPED or
  FAILED only - RUNNING and STARTING need timer history no scenario can yet
  declare - and moves only along STOPPED -> STARTING -> RUNNING -> STOPPED.
  FAILED is inert: no output, no transition in or out, and not an execution
  failure. Failure and recovery causes are T035's.
- **Site-owned control policy, and no trigger of the model's own.** The
  controller requests RUN only when the state of charge at T is at or below
  the Site's frozen `start-soc-threshold`, and STOP only when the generator
  is RUNNING, the Site's `minimum-runtime` has elapsed since it became
  RUNNING and the state of charge is at or above `stop-soc-threshold`.
- **The Site's frozen source priority, unsubstituted.** Sources serve load in
  that order (Kobo: PV, battery, generator), so a running generator serves
  what the sources ahead of it do not. It may also charge the battery, only
  when the battery is not discharging, within the PCS charge limit and the
  battery's headroom; the rating is a ceiling, never a command.
- **Fuel is checked before energy is accepted**, and falls by the Foundation's
  specific fuel consumption times accepted generator energy - to load and to
  the battery alike. The law is linear: no idle fuel and no start fuel, a
  simplification this pack discloses in every step's notes.

Lifecycle timing: transitions happen at step boundaries only. A start delay
that has elapsed is due in phase A, before the sample at T; a request emitted
at T moves the state in phase F, after the sample and the view. Start delay
and minimum runtime must be step-aligned: one ending inside a step is refused
rather than rounded.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

    EffectiveControl,

    ViewEntry,
)

GENERATOR_STATE = "generator-state"

STOP_THRESHOLD = "generator-stop-soc-threshold"
MINIMUM_RUNTIME = "generator-minimum-runtime"

STOPPED, STARTING, RUNNING, FAILED = "STOPPED", "STARTING", "RUNNING", "FAILED"

GENERATOR_LIFECYCLE = DiscreteStateSpec(
    state_key=GENERATOR_STATE,
    scope="COMPONENT",
    allowed_values=(STOPPED, STARTING, RUNNING, FAILED),
    initialization_owner="SCENARIO",
    initial_values_supported=frozenset({STOPPED, FAILED}),
    transitions=frozenset(
        {(STOPPED, STARTING), (STARTING, RUNNING), (RUNNING, STOPPED)}
    ),
    observable_signals=("generator-status (reported status is T025b's)",),
    statement=(
        "The generator's physical lifecycle state. STOPPED starts on a Site "
        "policy request and is STARTING for the frozen start delay, "
        "delivering nothing; RUNNING delivers up to its rating; a Site "
        "policy stop returns it to STOPPED. FAILED is declared and inert: "
        "failure and recovery causes are refused until T035. RUNNING and "
        "STARTING are not supported at run start, because they need timer "
        "history no scenario can declare yet."
    ),
)


def _minutes(what: str):
    def accept(value: Fraction, *_: object) -> Fraction:
        if value < 0 or value.denominator != 1:
            raise PhysicallyUnacceptable(
                f"{what} of {value} min is not a whole, non-negative number of "
                "minutes"
            )
        return value

    return accept


def _positive(what: str, unit: str):
    def accept(value: Fraction, *_: object) -> Fraction:
        if value <= 0:
            raise PhysicallyUnacceptable(f"{what} of {value} {unit} is not positive")
        return value

    return accept


def _fuel_volume(value: Fraction, *_: object) -> Fraction:
    if value < 0:
        raise PhysicallyUnacceptable(
            f"a stored volume of {value} litres is not a volume a tank holds"
        )
    return value

    ),

# --- The plant --------------------------------------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _Generator:
    generator: str
    tank: str
    rated: Fraction
    sfc: Fraction
    start_threshold: Fraction
    stop_threshold: Fraction
    minimum_runtime: int
    start_delay: int
    tank_capacity: Fraction
    #: The Site's source priority over the three sources this model runs.
    order: tuple[str, ...]

    @property
    def state_address(self) -> str:
        return f"{GENERATOR_STATE}@{self.generator}"

    @property
    def fuel_address(self) -> str:
        return f"{FUEL_TANK_VOLUME}@{self.tank}"


def _generator(context: StepContext, plant: daytime._Plant) -> _Generator:
    answers = {
        key: daytime._one(context, key, what)
        for key, what in (
            (GENERATOR_RATED_POWER, "generator"),
            (GENERATOR_SPECIFIC_CONSUMPTION, "generator"),
            (START_THRESHOLD, "generator"),
            (STOP_THRESHOLD, "generator"),
            (MINIMUM_RUNTIME, "generator"),
            (START_DELAY, "generator"),
            (FUEL_TANK_CAPACITY, "fuel tank"),
        )
    }
    generator = daytime._component(answers[GENERATOR_RATED_POWER][0])
    for key in (
        GENERATOR_SPECIFIC_CONSUMPTION,
        START_THRESHOLD,
        STOP_THRESHOLD,
        MINIMUM_RUNTIME,
        START_DELAY,
    ):
        if daytime._component(answers[key][0]) != generator:
            raise CouplingRefused(
                "TOPOLOGY_INCONSISTENT",
                answers[key][0],
                f"{key} and the rating name two different generators",
            )
    tank = daytime._component(answers[FUEL_TANK_CAPACITY][0])
    value = {key: item[1] for key, item in answers.items()}

    sources = context.site_control(daytime.SOURCE_PRIORITY)
    modelled = {plant.pv: "pv", plant.battery: "battery", generator: "generator"}
    order = tuple(modelled[m] for m in sources.members if m in modelled)
    if sorted(order) != ["battery", "generator", "pv"]:
        raise CouplingRefused(
            "TOPOLOGY_INCONSISTENT",
            f"site-control:{daytime.SOURCE_PRIORITY}",
            "the source priority must name this run's PV array, battery and "
            "generator exactly once each; this model never substitutes an order",
            f"it names {list(sources.members)}",
        )
    daytime._require_bus(
        context,
        (
            ("GENERATION", generator, None),
            ("FUEL_SUPPLY", tank, generator),
        ),
    )
    return _Generator(
        generator=generator,
        tank=tank,
        rated=value[GENERATOR_RATED_POWER],
        sfc=value[GENERATOR_SPECIFIC_CONSUMPTION],
        start_threshold=value[START_THRESHOLD],
        stop_threshold=value[STOP_THRESHOLD],
        minimum_runtime=int(value[MINIMUM_RUNTIME]),
        start_delay=int(value[START_DELAY]),
        tank_capacity=value[FUEL_TANK_CAPACITY],
        order=order,
    )


def _both(context: StepContext) -> tuple[daytime._Plant, _Generator]:
    plant = daytime._plant(context)
    return plant, _generator(context, plant)


def _state(context: StepContext, gen: _Generator):
    found = context.discrete_value(gen.state_address)
    if found is None:
        raise CouplingRefused(
            "INITIAL_STATE_UNANSWERED",
            gen.state_address,
            "the generator's lifecycle state at run start is scenario-owned and "
            "the run does not state it; it is never assumed",
        )
    return found


def _refuse_a_deadline_inside_the_step(
    context: StepContext, address: str, deadline: int, what: str
) -> None:
    if context.from_offset < deadline < context.to_offset:
        raise CouplingRefused(
            "PHYSICAL_RESOLUTION_FAILURE",
            address,
            f"the {what} ends at offset {deadline}, inside the step "
            f"[{context.from_offset}, {context.to_offset}); lifecycle "
            "transitions happen at step boundaries only, so it must be a whole "
            "multiple of the step",
        )


def _stop_threshold_at(context: StepContext, gen: _Generator) -> tuple[Fraction, str, str]:
    """The stop threshold in effect over this step, and where it comes from.

    T025b (`a-control-override-replaces-its-baseline`). A scenario override
    replaces the frozen Foundation baseline absolutely inside its window and
    nowhere else. Only `mini-grid` v2 declares the override, so under v1 there
    is never an exposure here and the baseline always applies. The controller
    reads policy at T only, so an override that begins or ends inside the step
    is refused rather than applied early or split (setup blocks it first).
    """
    address = f"{STOP_THRESHOLD}@{gen.generator}"
    exposures = context.exposures_of(address)
    for exposure in exposures:
        if (
            exposure.from_offset != context.from_offset
            or exposure.to_offset != context.to_offset
        ):
            raise CouplingRefused(
                "PHYSICAL_RESOLUTION_FAILURE",
                address,
                f"the stop-threshold override {exposure.event_id} covers "
                f"[{exposure.from_offset}, {exposure.to_offset}) of the step "
                f"[{context.from_offset}, {context.to_offset}); generator policy "
                "is read at step boundaries only, so an override window must "
                "start and end on them",
            )
    found = daytime._level_at(context, address, context.from_offset)
    if found is None:
        return (
            gen.stop_threshold,
            "BASELINE",
            "frozen Foundation answer (stop-soc-threshold)",
        )
    value, event_id = found
    if not gen.start_threshold < value <= HUNDRED:
        raise CouplingRefused(
            "PHYSICAL_RESOLUTION_FAILURE",
            address,
            f"the stop-threshold override {event_id} of {value} % is not above "
            f"the start threshold {gen.start_threshold} % and at most 100 %, so "
            "the Site policy would have no band",
        )
    return value, "OVERRIDE", f"scenario override {event_id}"


# --- The callables ----------------------------------------------------------


def prepare(context: StepContext) -> None:
    daytime.prepare(context)
    plant, gen = _both(context)
    _state(context, gen)
    if gen.start_threshold >= gen.stop_threshold:
        raise CouplingRefused(
            "PHYSICAL_RESOLUTION_FAILURE",
            f"{STOP_THRESHOLD}@{gen.generator}",
            f"the start threshold {gen.start_threshold} % is not below the stop "
            f"threshold {gen.stop_threshold} %, so the Site policy has no band",
        )
    if gen.start_delay <= 0:
        raise CouplingRefused(
            "PHYSICAL_RESOLUTION_FAILURE",
            f"{START_DELAY}@{gen.generator}",
            "the start delay must be positive: STARTING lasts at least one step",
        )
    fuel = context.stock(gen.fuel_address)
    if fuel is None:
        raise CouplingRefused(
            "INITIAL_STATE_UNANSWERED",
            gen.fuel_address,
            "the fuel in the tank at run start is scenario-owned and not stated",
        )
    if fuel > gen.tank_capacity:
        raise CouplingRefused(
            "PHYSICAL_RESOLUTION_FAILURE",
            gen.fuel_address,
            f"{fuel} L at run start exceeds the tank capacity {gen.tank_capacity} L",
        )


def due(context: StepContext) -> tuple[TransitionRequest, ...]:
    """Phase A: a start delay that has elapsed makes STARTING -> RUNNING due."""
    plant, gen = _both(context)
    current = _state(context, gen)
    if current.value != STARTING or current.since_offset is None:
        return ()
    deadline = current.since_offset + gen.start_delay
    if deadline > context.from_offset:
        _refuse_a_deadline_inside_the_step(
            context, gen.state_address, deadline, "start delay"
        )
        return ()
    return (
        TransitionRequest(
            address=gen.state_address,
            next=RUNNING,
            cause=(
                f"start delay of {gen.start_delay} min elapsed since STARTING "
                f"at offset {current.since_offset}"
            ),
        ),
    )


def view(context: StepContext) -> ControllerView:
    base = daytime.view(context)
    plant, gen = _both(context)
    current = _state(context, gen)
    at = context.from_offset
    running_for = (
        Fraction(at - current.since_offset)
        if current.value == RUNNING and current.since_offset is not None
        else None
    )
    if running_for is not None:
        _refuse_a_deadline_inside_the_step(
            context,
            gen.state_address,
            current.since_offset + gen.minimum_runtime,
            "minimum runtime",
        )
    ranks = [entry for entry in base.entries if entry.name != "source-priority-rank"]
    ids = {"pv": plant.pv, "battery": plant.battery, "generator": gen.generator}
    entries = ranks + [
        ViewEntry("generator-state", gen.state_address, current.value, DISCRETE_UNIT),
        ViewEntry("generator-running-for", gen.state_address, running_for, "min"),
        ViewEntry("generator-rated-power", f"{GENERATOR_RATED_POWER}@{gen.generator}", gen.rated, "kW"),
        ViewEntry("start-soc-threshold", f"{START_THRESHOLD}@{gen.generator}", gen.start_threshold, "%"),
        ViewEntry("stop-soc-threshold", f"{STOP_THRESHOLD}@{gen.generator}", _stop_threshold_at(context, gen)[0], "%"),
        ViewEntry("minimum-runtime", f"{MINIMUM_RUNTIME}@{gen.generator}", Fraction(gen.minimum_runtime), "min"),
        ViewEntry("fuel-in-tank", gen.fuel_address, context.stock(gen.fuel_address), "L"),
    ]
    for rank, source in enumerate(gen.order, start=1):
        entries.append(ViewEntry("source-priority-rank", ids[source], Fraction(rank), "rank"))
    return ControllerView(
        at_offset_minutes=base.at_offset_minutes,
        for_until_offset=base.for_until_offset,
        entries=tuple(entries),
    )


def _entry(view: ControllerView, name: str) -> ViewEntry:
    return next(item for item in view.entries if item.name == name)


def decide(view: ControllerView) -> ControlIntent:
    """Site policy for the generator, then the battery request, from the view."""
    soc_entry = _entry(view, "state-of-charge")
    soc = soc_entry.value
    reserve = view.value("reserve-in-effect")
    state_entry = _entry(view, "generator-state")
    state = state_entry.value
    running_for = view.value("generator-running-for")
    start = view.value("start-soc-threshold")
    stop = view.value("stop-soc-threshold")
    minimum = view.value("minimum-runtime")

    if state == STOPPED or state == FAILED:
        if soc <= start:
            run, why = RUN, (
                f"Site policy start: state of charge {soc} % is at or below "
                f"the start threshold {start} %"
            )
        else:
            run, why = STOP, (
                f"stay stopped: state of charge {soc} % is above the start "
                f"threshold {start} %"
            )
    elif state == STARTING:
        run, why = RUN, "keep starting: the start request stands through the start delay"
    elif running_for is not None and running_for >= minimum and soc >= stop:
        run, why = STOP, (
            f"Site policy stop: RUNNING {running_for} min, at least the minimum "
            f"runtime {minimum} min, and state of charge {soc} % is at or above "
            f"the stop threshold {stop} %"
        )
    elif running_for is not None and running_for < minimum:
        run, why = RUN, (
            f"keep running: RUNNING {running_for} min, below the minimum runtime "
            f"{minimum} min"
        )
    else:
        run, why = RUN, (
            f"keep running: state of charge {soc} % is below the stop threshold "
            f"{stop} %"
        )

    available = {
        "pv": view.value("pv-available"),
        "battery": Fraction(0),
        "generator": (
            view.value("generator-rated-power")
            if state == RUNNING and run == RUN
            else Fraction(0)
        ),
    }
    demand = sum(
        (item.value for item in view.entries if item.name == "load-demand"),
        Fraction(0),
    )
    ranks = {
        item.address: item.value
        for item in view.entries
        if item.name == "source-priority-rank"
    }
    battery_id = soc_entry.address.split("@", 1)[1]
    generator_id = state_entry.address.split("@", 1)[1]
    by_rank = sorted(ranks, key=lambda address: ranks[address])
    names = {battery_id: "battery", generator_id: "generator"}
    order = [names.get(address, "pv") for address in by_rank]
    ahead = sum(
        (available[source] for source in order[: order.index("battery")]),
        Fraction(0),
    )
    deficit = demand - ahead
    everything = available["pv"] + available["generator"]
    if deficit > 0 and soc > reserve:
        requested, reason = deficit, (
            f"discharge {deficit} kW: demand {demand} kW exceeds the {ahead} kW "
            f"the sources ahead of the battery in the Site's priority offer, and "
            f"the state of charge {soc} % is above the reserve {reserve} %"
        )
    elif everything > demand and soc < HUNDRED:
        requested, reason = demand - everything, (
            f"charge from the {everything - demand} kW the running sources "
            f"offer beyond demand {demand} kW"
        )
    elif deficit > 0:
        requested, reason = Fraction(0), (
            f"no discharge: the state of charge {soc} % is not above the "
            f"reserve {reserve} % in effect"
        )
    else:
        requested, reason = Fraction(0), "no battery power is called for"
    return ControlIntent(
        from_offset=view.at_offset_minutes,
        to_offset=view.for_until_offset,
        requests=(
            ControlRequest(
                address=soc_entry.address,
                quantity="battery-power",
                requested=requested,
                unit="kW",
                reason=reason,
            ),
            ControlRequest(
                address=state_entry.address,
                quantity="generator-run",
                requested=run,
                unit=DISCRETE_UNIT,
                reason=why,
            ),
        ),
    )


def _generator_request(intent: ControlIntent) -> ControlRequest:
    return next(item for item in intent.requests if item.quantity == "generator-run")


def commit(context: StepContext, intent: ControlIntent) -> tuple[TransitionRequest, ...]:
    """Phase F, before resolution: what the request just emitted at T causes."""
    plant, gen = _both(context)
    current = _state(context, gen).value
    request = _generator_request(intent)
    ref = f"control-intent@{intent.from_offset}:generator-run"
    if current == STOPPED and request.requested == RUN:
        return (TransitionRequest(gen.state_address, STARTING, request.reason, ref),)
    if current == RUNNING and request.requested == STOP:
        return (TransitionRequest(gen.state_address, STOPPED, request.reason, ref),)
    return ()


def resolve(context: StepContext, intent: ControlIntent) -> AcceptedFlowSet:
    """What the world accepts over the step, segment by segment."""
    plant, gen = _both(context)
    battery_request = intent.requests[0]
    run_request = _generator_request(intent)
    requested = battery_request.requested
    state = _state(context, gen)
    eta = plant.efficiency
    capacity = plant.capacity
    stored = context.stock(plant.soc_address) * capacity / HUNDRED
    stored_at_start = stored
    fuel = context.stock(gen.fuel_address)
    fuel_at_start = fuel

    totals: dict[str, Fraction] = {}
    per_load = {load: [Fraction(0), Fraction(0)] for load in plant.loads}
    limited: list[str] = []
    generator_limited: list[str] = []
    effective: list[EffectiveControl] = []
    segments = daytime._segments(context)

    def add(flow_id: str, energy: Fraction) -> None:
        totals[flow_id] = totals.get(flow_id, Fraction(0)) + energy

    def note(into: list[str], reason: str) -> None:
        if reason not in into:
            into.append(reason)

    if state.value == STARTING:
        note(generator_limited, f"STARTING: no output during the {gen.start_delay} min start delay")
    elif state.value == FAILED:
        note(generator_limited, "FAILED: inert, no output (failure causes are T035's)")
    elif state.value == STOPPED:
        note(generator_limited, "STOPPED: no output")

    for start, end in segments:
        hours = Fraction(end - start, 60)
        irradiance = daytime._required_level(context, daytime.IRRADIANCE_ADDRESS, start)
        potential, available = daytime._pv_available(plant, irradiance)
        reserve, origin, provenance = daytime._reserve_at(context, plant, start)
        effective.append(
            EffectiveControl(plant.reserve_address, start, end, reserve, "%", origin, provenance)
        )
        demands = {
            load: daytime._required_level(context, plant.load_address(load), start)
            for load in plant.loads
        }
        demand = sum(demands.values(), Fraction(0))

        generator_cap = Fraction(0)
        if state.value == RUNNING:
            by_fuel = fuel / gen.sfc / hours
            generator_cap = min(gen.rated, by_fuel)
            if by_fuel < gen.rated:
                note(generator_limited, f"fuel in tank ({fuel} L at the segment start)")

        discharge_cap = Fraction(0)
        if requested > 0:
            above_reserve = stored - reserve * capacity / HUNDRED
            by_energy = max(above_reserve, Fraction(0)) * eta / hours
            discharge_cap = min(requested, plant.discharge_limit, by_energy)
            if discharge_cap == plant.discharge_limit and requested > plant.discharge_limit:
                note(limited, f"pcs-discharge-limit {plant.discharge_limit} kW")
            if by_energy < min(requested, plant.discharge_limit):
                note(limited, f"energy above the reserve in effect ({reserve} %)")
        charge_cap = Fraction(0)
        if requested < 0:
            by_energy = (capacity - stored) / (eta * hours)
            charge_cap = min(-requested, plant.charge_limit, by_energy)
            if charge_cap == plant.charge_limit and -requested > plant.charge_limit:
                note(limited, f"pcs-charge-limit {plant.charge_limit} kW")
            if by_energy < min(-requested, plant.charge_limit):
                note(limited, "headroom below full")

        supply = {"pv": available, "battery": discharge_cap, "generator": generator_cap}
        served_total = min(demand, sum(supply.values(), Fraction(0)))
        remaining = served_total
        to_load = {"pv": Fraction(0), "battery": Fraction(0), "generator": Fraction(0)}
        for source in gen.order:
            take = min(supply[source], remaining)
            to_load[source] = take
            remaining -= take

        left = served_total
        for load in plant.loads:
            served = min(demands[load], left)
            left -= served
            per_load[load][0] += demands[load] * hours
            per_load[load][1] += served * hours

        # Charging: only while the battery is not discharging, from what the
        # charging sources have beyond load, in the Site's source order.
        to_battery = {"pv": Fraction(0), "generator": Fraction(0)}
        if to_load["battery"] == 0:
            room = charge_cap
            for source in gen.order:
                if source == "battery":
                    continue
                take = min(supply[source] - to_load[source], room)
                to_battery[source] = take
                room -= take

        generator_energy = (to_load["generator"] + to_battery["generator"]) * hours
        burned = generator_energy * gen.sfc
        fuel -= burned
        charged_ac = (to_battery["pv"] + to_battery["generator"]) * hours
        stored_in = charged_ac * eta
        delivered = to_load["battery"] * hours
        taken_out = delivered / eta
        stored = stored + stored_in - taken_out
        curtailed = available - to_load["pv"] - to_battery["pv"]

        add("pv_potential", potential * hours)
        add("inverter_clipping", (potential - available) * hours)
        add("pv_to_load", to_load["pv"] * hours)
        add("pv_to_battery", to_battery["pv"] * hours)
        add("curtailed_pv", curtailed * hours)
        add("generator_output", generator_energy)
        add("generator_to_load", to_load["generator"] * hours)
        add("generator_to_battery", to_battery["generator"] * hours)
        add("battery_charge_stored", stored_in)
        add("charge_loss", charged_ac - stored_in)
        add("battery_discharge_taken", taken_out)
        add("battery_to_load", delivered)
        add("discharge_loss", taken_out - delivered)
        add("load_demand", demand * hours)
        add("unserved_load", (demand - served_total) * hours)

    step_hours = Fraction(context.to_offset - context.from_offset, 60)
    pv, inv, bat, pcs, g = plant.pv, plant.inverter, plant.battery, plant.pcs, gen.generator

    def flow(flow_id: str, source: str, sink: str) -> AcceptedFlow:
        energy = totals[flow_id]
        return AcceptedFlow(flow_id, source, sink, energy, energy / step_hours)

    flows = [
        flow("pv_potential", pv, inv),
        flow("inverter_clipping", inv, "clipped"),
        flow("pv_to_load", inv, "loads"),
        flow("pv_to_battery", inv, pcs),
        flow("curtailed_pv", inv, "curtailed"),
        flow("generator_output", g, "generator-output"),
        flow("generator_to_load", g, "loads"),
        flow("generator_to_battery", g, pcs),
        flow("battery_charge_stored", pcs, bat),
        flow("charge_loss", pcs, "losses"),
        flow("battery_discharge_taken", bat, pcs),
        flow("battery_to_load", pcs, "loads"),
        flow("discharge_loss", pcs, "losses"),
        flow("load_demand", "loads", "demand"),
        flow("unserved_load", "demand", "unserved"),
    ]
    loads: list[LoadService] = []
    for rank, load in enumerate(plant.loads, start=1):
        demand_energy, served_energy = per_load[load]
        unserved_energy = demand_energy - served_energy
        flows.append(AcceptedFlow(f"demand:{load}", load, "demand", demand_energy, demand_energy / step_hours))
        flows.append(AcceptedFlow(f"served:{load}", "loads", load, served_energy, served_energy / step_hours))
        flows.append(AcceptedFlow(f"unserved:{load}", load, "unserved", unserved_energy, unserved_energy / step_hours))
        loads.append(
            LoadService(
                address=plant.load_address(load),
                priority_rank=rank,
                demand_energy=demand_energy,
                served_energy=served_energy,
                unserved_energy=unserved_energy,
                demand_power=demand_energy / step_hours,
                served_power=served_energy / step_hours,
                unserved_power=unserved_energy / step_hours,
            )
        )

    served_ids = tuple(f"served:{load}" for load in plant.loads)
    to_load_ids = ("pv_to_load", "battery_to_load", "generator_to_load")
    balances = [
        BalanceIdentity(
            "pv-potential",
            ("pv_potential",),
            ("pv_to_load", "pv_to_battery", "curtailed_pv", "inverter_clipping"),
        ),
        BalanceIdentity(
            "generator-output", ("generator_output",), ("generator_to_load", "generator_to_battery")
        ),
        BalanceIdentity(
            "battery-charge",
            ("pv_to_battery", "generator_to_battery"),
            ("battery_charge_stored", "charge_loss"),
        ),
        BalanceIdentity(
            "battery-discharge",
            ("battery_discharge_taken",),
            ("battery_to_load", "discharge_loss"),
        ),
        BalanceIdentity("load-service", ("load_demand",), to_load_ids + ("unserved_load",)),
        BalanceIdentity("served-by-load", to_load_ids, served_ids),
        BalanceIdentity(
            "site-sources-and-sinks",
            ("pv_potential", "battery_discharge_taken", "generator_output"),
            served_ids
            + (
                "curtailed_pv",
                "inverter_clipping",
                "battery_charge_stored",
                "charge_loss",
                "discharge_loss",
            ),
        ),
    ]
    for load in plant.loads:
        balances.append(
            BalanceIdentity(
                f"load:{load}", (f"demand:{load}",), (f"served:{load}", f"unserved:{load}")
            )
        )

    battery_net_ac = (
        totals["battery_to_load"] - totals["pv_to_battery"] - totals["generator_to_battery"]
    )
    accepted_power = battery_net_ac / step_hours
    if accepted_power != requested and not limited:
        limited.append("the demand or the sources across the step differed from the view at T")
    if state.value == RUNNING and totals["generator_output"] == 0:
        note(generator_limited, "RUNNING with nothing to absorb its output")

    return AcceptedFlowSet(
        from_offset=context.from_offset,
        to_offset=context.to_offset,
        segments=segments,
        flows=tuple(flows),
        controls=(
            AcceptedControl(
                address=battery_request.address,
                quantity=battery_request.quantity,
                requested=requested,
                accepted=accepted_power,
                unit="kW",
                limited_by=tuple(limited),
            ),
            AcceptedControl(
                address=run_request.address,
                quantity=run_request.quantity,
                requested=run_request.requested,
                accepted=state.value,
                unit=DISCRETE_UNIT,
                limited_by=tuple(generator_limited),
            ),
        ),
        loads=tuple(loads),
        effective_controls=tuple(effective),
        balances=tuple(balances),
        stock_accounts=(
            StockAccount(
                address=plant.soc_address,
                delta=(stored - stored_at_start) * HUNDRED / capacity,
                energy_per_unit=capacity / HUNDRED,
                inflows=("battery_charge_stored",),
                outflows=("battery_discharge_taken",),
            ),
            StockAccount(
                address=gen.fuel_address,
                delta=fuel - fuel_at_start,
                energy_per_unit=1 / gen.sfc,
                inflows=(),
                outflows=("generator_output",),
            ),
        ),
        interval_energies=tuple(
            sorted(
                [
                    (f"{daytime.PV_AC_POWER}@{inv}", totals["pv_to_load"] + totals["pv_to_battery"]),
                    (f"{daytime.BATTERY_AC_POWER}@{pcs}", battery_net_ac),
                ]
                + [
                    (f"{daytime.LOAD_SERVED_POWER}@{load}", per_load[load][1])
                    for load in plant.loads
                ]
            )
        ),
        notes=(LINEAR_FUEL_NOTE,),
    )


MINI_GRID_COUPLING = Coupling(
    statement=(
        "The daytime controller and cascade, plus a generator the Site's "
        "frozen policy starts and stops, dispatched in the Site's frozen "
        "source priority, burning fuel for accepted energy."
    ),
    prepare=prepare,
    view=view,
    decide=decide,
    resolve=resolve,
    due=due,
    commit=commit,
)

MINI_GRID_MODEL = ModelSpec(
    model_profile_id="mini-grid",
    model_profile_version=1,
    statement=(
        "Models one PV array behind one inverter, one battery behind one PCS, "
        "any number of addressed loads, and one generator with its fuel tank "
        "and lifecycle, under the Site's frozen control policy."
    ),
    handlers=daytime.HANDLERS + GENERATOR_HANDLERS,
    laws=(),
    component_relations=daytime.RELATIONS + GENERATOR_RELATIONS,
    coupling=MINI_GRID_COUPLING,
    measured_interval_states=frozenset(
        {daytime.PV_AC_POWER, daytime.BATTERY_AC_POWER, daytime.LOAD_SERVED_POWER}
    ),
    discrete_states=(GENERATOR_LIFECYCLE,),
)


# --- `mini-grid` v2 (T025b) ------------------------------------------------


STOP_THRESHOLD_OVERRIDE = StateHandler(
    handler_id="generator-stop-soc-threshold-override-forcing",
    state_key=STOP_THRESHOLD,
    scope="COMPONENT",
    role="FORCING_INPUT",
    kind="FORCING",
    dimension="FRACTION",
    statement=(
        "A scenario override of the generator's stop threshold, replacing the "
        "Site's frozen baseline inside its step-aligned window only."
    ),
    consume=daytime._forced_level("a stop threshold override", "%"),
)


def resolve_v2(context: StepContext, intent: ControlIntent) -> AcceptedFlowSet:
    """v1's resolution, plus the stop threshold in effect over the step.

    The effective stop threshold is recorded beside the reserve (after it, so
    the reserve stays the first effective control) so the time-valid policy a
    run executed is reconstructible from its trajectory alone.

    Phase 2 (criterion 6): the generator's accepted output energy over the step
    is also an interval measurement at the generator's address, so a
    `generator-output-power` INTERVAL_SIGNAL reports the mean power over the
    completed step - the same energy the fuel stock was debited for, never a
    power read at an instant. v1 is untouched: its runs keep their interval
    measurements and digests.
    """
    accepted = resolve(context, intent)
    plant, gen = _both(context)
    value, origin, provenance = _stop_threshold_at(context, gen)
    generator_energy = next(
        flow.energy
        for flow in accepted.flows
        if flow.flow_id == "generator_output"
    )
    return replace(
        accepted,
        interval_energies=tuple(
            sorted(
                accepted.interval_energies
                + ((f"{GENERATOR_OUTPUT_POWER}@{gen.generator}", generator_energy),)
            )
        ),
        effective_controls=accepted.effective_controls
        + (
            EffectiveControl(
                f"{STOP_THRESHOLD}@{gen.generator}",
                context.from_offset,
                context.to_offset,
                value,
                "%",
                origin,
                provenance,
            ),
        ),
    )


MINI_GRID_V2_COUPLING = replace(MINI_GRID_COUPLING, resolve=resolve_v2)

MINI_GRID_V2_MODEL = replace(
    MINI_GRID_MODEL,
    model_profile_version=2,
    statement=(
        MINI_GRID_MODEL.statement
        + " A scenario may override the battery reserve and the generator's "
        "stop threshold across a window; each replaces its frozen baseline "
        "there only."
    ),
    handlers=MINI_GRID_MODEL.handlers + (STOP_THRESHOLD_OVERRIDE,),
    coupling=MINI_GRID_V2_COUPLING,
    measured_interval_states=MINI_GRID_MODEL.measured_interval_states
    | {GENERATOR_OUTPUT_POWER},
)


# --- `mini-grid` v3 (T026) ------------------------------------------------

#: What the PV inverter reports it COULD deliver over the completed step: the
#: array's potential after the inverter's own limit. Not what was used - that is
#: `pv-ac-power` - so a consumer can see curtailment without reading a flow.
PV_AVAILABLE_POWER = "pv-available-power"

#: What the battery's management system reports it could discharge over the
#: completed step, as AC power at the PCS: the PCS limit, or less when the
#: energy stored at the step's start could not sustain it. It knows nothing of
#: the Site's reserve policy - that is a control, published as a policy record.
BATTERY_DISCHARGE_CAPABILITY = "battery-discharge-capability"


def resolve_v3(context: StepContext, intent: ControlIntent) -> AcceptedFlowSet:
    """v2's resolution, plus the two capability measurements a device reports.

    Measurements only: no flow, stock, control or decision changes, so a v3 run
    and a v2 run of one document accept the same flows and decide the same
    requests. v1 and v2 are untouched; their interval measurements and digests
    are what they were (`D-2026-09-22-contract-version-scope`).
    """
    accepted = resolve_v2(context, intent)
    plant, _ = _both(context)
    energy = {flow.flow_id: flow.energy for flow in accepted.flows}
    available = energy["pv_potential"] - energy["inverter_clipping"]
    hours = Fraction(context.to_offset - context.from_offset, 60)
    stored = context.stock(plant.soc_address) * plant.capacity / 100
    deliverable = max(stored, Fraction(0)) * plant.efficiency / hours
    capability = min(plant.discharge_limit, deliverable)
    return replace(
        accepted,
        interval_energies=tuple(
            sorted(
                accepted.interval_energies
                + (
                    (f"{PV_AVAILABLE_POWER}@{plant.inverter}", available),
                    (
                        f"{BATTERY_DISCHARGE_CAPABILITY}@{plant.pcs}",
                        capability * hours,
                    ),
                )
            )
        ),
    )


MINI_GRID_V3_COUPLING = replace(MINI_GRID_V2_COUPLING, resolve=resolve_v3)

MINI_GRID_V3_MODEL = replace(
    MINI_GRID_V2_MODEL,
    model_profile_version=3,
    statement=(
        MINI_GRID_V2_MODEL.statement
        + " It also measures, over each completed step, the PV inverter's "
        "available power and the battery's discharge capability, so a device "
        "can report capability beside output."
    ),
    coupling=MINI_GRID_V3_COUPLING,
    measured_interval_states=MINI_GRID_V2_MODEL.measured_interval_states
    | {PV_AVAILABLE_POWER, BATTERY_DISCHARGE_CAPABILITY},
)

def decide(view: ControllerView) -> ControlIntent:
    """Site policy for the generator, then the battery request, from the view."""
    soc_entry = _entry(view, "state-of-charge")
    soc = soc_entry.value
    reserve = view.value("reserve-in-effect")
    state_entry = _entry(view, "generator-state")
    state = state_entry.value
    running_for = view.value("generator-running-for")
    start = view.value("start-soc-threshold")
    stop = view.value("stop-soc-threshold")
    minimum = view.value("minimum-runtime")

    if state == STOPPED or state == FAILED:
        if soc <= start:
            run, why = RUN, (
                f"Site policy start: state of charge {soc} % is at or below "
                f"the start threshold {start} %"
            )
        else:
            run, why = STOP, (
                f"stay stopped: state of charge {soc} % is above the start "
                f"threshold {start} %"
            )
    elif state == STARTING:
        run, why = RUN, "keep starting: the start request stands through the start delay"
    elif running_for is not None and running_for >= minimum and soc >= stop:
        run, why = STOP, (
            f"Site policy stop: RUNNING {running_for} min, at least the minimum "
            f"runtime {minimum} min, and state of charge {soc} % is at or above "
            f"the stop threshold {stop} %"
        )
    elif running_for is not None and running_for < minimum:
        run, why = RUN, (
            f"keep running: RUNNING {running_for} min, below the minimum runtime "
            f"{minimum} min"
        )
    else:
        run, why = RUN, (
            f"keep running: state of charge {soc} % is below the stop threshold "
            f"{stop} %"
        )

    available = {
        "pv": view.value("pv-available"),
        "battery": Fraction(0),
        "generator": (
            view.value("generator-rated-power")
            if state == RUNNING and run == RUN
            else Fraction(0)
        ),
    }
    demand = sum(
        (item.value for item in view.entries if item.name == "load-demand"),
        Fraction(0),
    )
    ranks = {
        item.address: item.value
        for item in view.entries
        if item.name == "source-priority-rank"
    }
    battery_id = soc_entry.address.split("@", 1)[1]
    generator_id = state_entry.address.split("@", 1)[1]
    by_rank = sorted(ranks, key=lambda address: ranks[address])
    names = {battery_id: "battery", generator_id: "generator"}
    order = [names.get(address, "pv") for address in by_rank]
    ahead = sum(
        (available[source] for source in order[: order.index("battery")]),
        Fraction(0),
    )
    deficit = demand - ahead
    everything = available["pv"] + available["generator"]
    if deficit > 0 and soc > reserve:
        requested, reason = deficit, (
            f"discharge {deficit} kW: demand {demand} kW exceeds the {ahead} kW "

#: T026. `mini-grid` v3: v2 unchanged beside it, plus two measured interval
#: states - the PV inverter's available power and the battery's discharge
#: capability - so publication profile 5 can report capability beside output.
#: Measurements only: v3 accepts the same flows and decides the same requests as
#: v2 for one document. A new version rather than an edit of v2, because adding
#: state to an existing profile's trajectory would change frozen runs' digests.
MINI_GRID_V3_MODEL = replace(
    MINI_GRID_V2_MODEL,
    model_profile_version=3,
    statement=(
        MINI_GRID_V2_MODEL.statement
        + " Version three also measures, over each completed step, the PV "
        "inverter's available power and the battery's discharge capability."
    ),
)

#: The publication profile the Lab publishes through.
#:
#: It declares the three things nothing else may supply: how often a device
#: signal reports, which simulator source a run publishes as, and which
#: gateway it publishes through. All three are this profile's to declare and
#: nobody else's, which is why a run that selects a profile declaring none of
#: them is blocked rather than defaulted.
#:
#: Since T020B it declares a fourth thing: that it can model the reporting path
#: being unavailable. That is the one the shipped Fuel Loss Event needs, and it
#: belongs here rather than in the model profile because a sensor reporting
#: nothing changes nothing about the fuel. This profile OWNS the path, so
#: suppressing what travels along it is a capability of the path.
#:
#: ## Version two since T022, and the version moves because the content does
#:
#: `device_signals` is new, and it is what turns a sampled world into a reading:
#: which configured signals report which state, when, with what error and with
#: what reliability. A run freezes which publication profile answered and at
#: which version precisely so that the readings it produced can be reproduced
#: without the profile catalog being frozen with it. Leaving this at version one
#: would mean two builds claiming one version and generating different series,
#: which is the whole thing the version exists to rule out.
#:
#:
#: Six electrical reporting paths are added: the battery's state of charge,
#: the PCS's AC power, the PV inverter's AC power and one meter per load. A run
#: of an unchanged document against this profile generates a different series
#: than it would under two, so the version moves. A declared path whose state
#: the selected model does not produce is not a reporting path for that run -
#: a fuel run on a hybrid Site does not grow a column of empty battery readings.
#:
#: Two reporting paths, and both resolve against MG-001's own Foundation because
#: a profile declares a signal's identity and run setup says whether this
#: installation configures it. They are deliberately different in every field
#: that matters, because criterion 10's claim is about the key and a pair that
#: differed only in name would not test it:
#:
#: - `fuel-level-sensor`/`fuel-level` reports the tank's stored volume every
#:   fifteen minutes, half a litre low, losing one sample in twenty-five. It is
#:   the signal the shipped scenario's own declared observation source names, and
#:   the one the scenario's reporting gap silences across the removal.
#: - `generator-controller`/`ac-power` reports the average output over the span
#:   that just ended, hourly, with no error and losing nothing. The cadence and
#:   the span are different numbers on purpose: the cadence says when it publishes
#:   and the run's timestep says what span the measurement covers, which is what
#:   real telemetry does.
#:
#: **The dropout is on the fifteen-minute signal deliberately, and the reason is
#: about evidence rather than realism.** An hourly signal whose measurement exists
#: only while the generator runs has four valued samples in this interval, so a
#: dropout declared on it would draw four times and would quite likely select
#: none - and a stochastic mechanism that produced nothing would be a mechanism
#: whose test passes over an empty set. On the fifteen-minute signal it draws a
#: hundred and fifty-nine times and drops a visible handful, which is what makes
#: "adding an unrelated stream changes no existing stream" worth asserting.
LAB_PUBLICATION_PROFILE = PublicationProfile(
    publication_profile_id="simulator-lab-publication",
    publication_profile_version=3,
    display_name="Simulator Lab publication profile",
    statement=(
        "Declares which configured device signals report which world state, how "
        "often, with what bias and with what share of samples not arriving; the "
        "simulator source and gateway identities a run would publish through; "
        "and that a run may force the reporting path for a configured signal to "
        "be unavailable across a declared window. A site's foundation declares "
        "that a signal can report and declares its unit, and declares none of "
        "these; nothing derives them from a device or from how a site was "

Decision: require a versioned, reserve-aware mini-grid model/profile and a genuinely VERIFIED public Lab replay in S6-02b, before its owner walkthrough. This does not block S6-02a merge after its fresh independent review.
